]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNot equal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ NUM -lt NUM ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLess than\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ NUM -le NUM ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLess than or equal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ NUM -gt NUM ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGreater than\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ NUM -ge NUM ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGreater than or equal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e(( NUM \u0026lt; NUM ))\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLess than\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e(( NUM \u0026lt;= NUM ))\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLess than or equal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e(( NUM \u0026gt; NUM -z STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEmpty string\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ -n STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNot empty string\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ STR == STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEqual\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ STR = STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEqual (Same above)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ STR \u0026lt; STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLess than \u003cem\u003e(ASCII)\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ STR \u0026gt; STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGreater than \u003cem\u003e(ASCII)\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ STR != STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNot Equal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e[[ STR =~ STR ]]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRegexp\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv command\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e!!:gs/\u0026lt;FROM\u0026gt;/\u0026lt;TO\u0026gt;/\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReplace all occurrences of \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;FROM\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e to \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;TO\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e in most recent command\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e!$:t\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eExpand only basename from last parameter of most recent Bash","date: 2020-11-25 18:28:43","background: bg-[#3e4548]","tags:","  - shell","  - sh","  - echo","  - script","  - linux","categories:","  - Programming","  - Operating System","intro: This is a quick reference cheat sheet to getting started with linux bash shell scripting.","plugins:","  - copyCode","---","","## Getting Started","","### hello.sh","","```bash","#!/bin/bash","","VAR=\"world\"","echo \"Hello $VAR!\" # =\u003e Hello world!","```","","Execute the script","","```shell script","$ bash hello.sh","```","","### Variables","","```bash","NAME=\"John\"","","echo ${NAME}    # =\u003e John (Variables)","echo $NAME      # =\u003e John (Variables)","echo \"$NAME\"    # =\u003e John (Variables)","echo '$NAME'    # =\u003e $NAME (Exact string)","echo \"${NAME}!\" # =\u003e John! (Variables)","","NAME = \"John\"   # =\u003e Error (about space)","```","","### Comments","","```bash","# This is an inline Bash comment.","```","","```bash",": '","This is a","very neat comment","in bash","'","```","","Multi-line comments use `:'` to open and `'` to close","","### Arguments {.row-span-2}","","| Expression  | Description                           |","| ----------- | ------------------------------------- |","| `$1` … `$9` | Parameter 1 ... 9                     |","| `$0`        | Name of the script itself             |","| `$1`        | First argument                        |","| `${10}`     | Positional parameter 10               |","| `$#`        | Number of arguments                   |","| `$$`        | Process id of the shell               |","| `$*`        | All arguments                         |","| `$@`        | All arguments, starting from first    |","| `$-`        | Current options                       |","| `$_`        | Last argument of the previous command |","","See: [Special parameters](http://wiki.bash-hackers.org/syntax/shellvars#special_parameters_and_shell_variables)","","### Functions","","```bash","get_name() {","    echo \"John\"","}","","echo \"You are $(get_name)\"","```","","See: [Functions](#bash-functions)","","### Conditionals {#conditionals-example}","","```bash","if [[ -z \"$string\" ]]; then","    echo \"String is empty\"","elif [[ -n \"$string\" ]]; then","    echo \"String is not empty\"","fi","```","","See: [Conditionals](#bash-conditionals)","","### Brace expansion","","```bash","echo {A,B}.js","```","","---","","| Expression | Description         |","| ---------- | ------------------- |","| `{A,B}`    | Same as `A B`       |","| `{A,B}.js` | Same as `A.js B.js` |","| `{1..5}`   | Same as `1 2 3 4 5` |","","See: [Brace expansion](http://wiki.bash-hackers.org/syntax/expansion/brace)","","### Shell execution","","```bash","# =\u003e I'm in /path/of/current","echo \"I'm in $(PWD)\"","","# Same as:","echo \"I'm in `pwd`\"","```","","See: [Command substitution](http://wiki.bash-hackers.org/syntax/expansion/cmdsubst)","","## Bash Parameter expansions","","### Syntax {.row-span-2}","","| Code              | Description         |","| ----------------- | ------------------- |","| `${FOO%suffix}`   | Remove suffix       |","| `${FOO#prefix}`   | Remove prefix       |","| `${FOO%%suffix}`  | Remove long suffix  |","| `${FOO##prefix}`  | Remove long prefix  |","| `${FOO/from/to}`  | Replace first match |","| `${FOO//from/to}` | Replace all         |","| `${FOO/%from/to}` | Replace suffix      |","| `${FOO/#from/to}` | Replace prefix      |","","#### Substrings","","| Expression      | Description                    |","| --------------- | ------------------------------ |","| `${FOO:0:3}`    | Substring _(position, length)_ |","| `${FOO:(-3):3}` | Substring from the right       |","","#### Length","","| Expression | Description      |","| ---------- | ---------------- |","| `${#FOO}`  | Length of `$FOO` |","","#### Default values","","| Expression        | Description                              |","| ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- |","| `${FOO:-val}`     | `$FOO`, or `val` if unset                |","| `${FOO:=val}`     | Set `$FOO` to `val` if unset             |","| `${FOO:+val}`     | `val` if `$FOO` is set                   |","| `${FOO:?message}` | Show message and exit if `$FOO` is unset |","","### Substitution","","```bash","echo ${food:-Cake}  #=\u003e $food or \"Cake\"","```","","```bash","STR=\"/path/to/foo.cpp\"","echo ${STR%.cpp}    # /path/to/foo","echo ${STR%.cpp}.o  # /path/to/foo.o","echo ${STR%/*}      # /path/to","","echo ${STR##*.}     # cpp (extension)","echo ${STR##*/}     # foo.cpp (basepath)","","echo ${STR#*/}      # path/to/foo.cpp","echo ${STR##*/}     # foo.cpp","","echo ${STR/foo/bar} # /path/to/bar.cpp","```","","### Slicing","","```bash","name=\"John\"","echo ${name}           # =\u003e John","echo ${name:0:2}       # =\u003e Jo","echo ${name::2}        # =\u003e Jo","echo ${name::-1}       # =\u003e Joh","echo ${name:(-1)}      # =\u003e n","echo ${name:(-2)}      # =\u003e hn","echo ${name:(-2):2}    # =\u003e hn","","length=2","echo ${name:0:length}  # =\u003e Jo","```","","See: [Parameter expansion](http://wiki.bash-hackers.org/syntax/pe)","","### basepath \u0026 dirpath","","```bash","SRC=\"/path/to/foo.cpp\"","```","","```bash","BASEPATH=${SRC##*/}","echo $BASEPATH  # =\u003e \"foo.cpp\"","","","DIRPATH=${SRC%$BASEPATH}","echo $DIRPATH   # =\u003e \"/path/to/\"","```","","### Transform","","```bash","STR=\"HELLO WORLD!\"","echo ${STR,}   # =\u003e hELLO WORLD!","echo ${STR,,}  # =\u003e hello world!","","STR=\"hello world!\"","echo ${STR^}   # =\u003e Hello world!","echo ${STR^^}  # =\u003e HELLO WORLD!","","ARR=(hello World)","echo \"${ARR[@],}\" # =\u003e hello world","echo \"${ARR[@]^}\" # =\u003e Hello World","```","","## Bash Arrays","","### Defining arrays","","```bash","Fruits=('Apple' 'Banana' 'Orange')","","Fruits[0]=\"Apple\"","Fruits[1]=\"Banana\"","Fruits[2]=\"Orange\"","","ARRAY1=(foo{1..2}) # =\u003e foo1 foo2","ARRAY2=({A..D})    # =\u003e A B C D","","# Merge =\u003e foo1 foo2 A B C D","ARRAY3=(${ARRAY1[@]} ${ARRAY2[@]})","","# declare construct","declare -a Numbers=(1 2 3)","Numbers+=(4 5) # Append =\u003e 1 2 3 4 5","```","","### Indexing","","| -                  | -             |","| ------------------ | ------------- |","| `${Fruits[0]}`     | First element |","| `${Fruits[-1]}`    | Last element  |","| `${Fruits[*]}`     | All elements  |","| `${Fruits[@]}`     | All elements  |","| `${#Fruits[@]}`    | Number of all |","| `${#Fruits}`       | Length of 1st |","| `${#Fruits[3]}`    | Length of nth |","| `${Fruits[@]:3:2}` | Range         |","| `${!Fruits[@]}`    | Keys of all   |","","### Iteration","","```bash","Fruits=('Apple' 'Banana' 'Orange')","","for e in \"${Fruits[@]}\"; do","    echo $e","done","```","","#### With index","","```bash","for i in \"${!Fruits[@]}\"; do","  printf \"%s\\t%s\\n\" \"$i\" \"${Fruits[$i]}\"","done","","```","","### Operations {.col-span-2}","","```bash","Fruits=(\"${Fruits[@]}\" \"Watermelon\")     # Push","Fruits+=('Watermelon')                   # Also Push","Fruits=( ${Fruits[@]/Ap*/} )             # Remove by regex match","unset Fruits[2]                          # Remove one item","Fruits=(\"${Fruits[@]}\")                  # Duplicate","Fruits=(\"${Fruits[@]}\" \"${Veggies[@]}\")  # Concatenate","lines=(`cat \"logfile\"`)                  # Read from file","```","","### Arrays as arguments","","```bash","function extract()","{","    local -n myarray=$1","    local idx=$2","    echo \"${myarray[$idx]}\"","}","Fruits=('Apple' 'Banana' 'Orange')","extract Fruits 2     # =\u003e Orangle","```","","## Bash Dictionaries","","### Defining","","```bash","declare -A sounds","```","","```bash","sounds[dog]=\"bark\"","sounds[cow]=\"moo\"","sounds[bird]=\"tweet\"","sounds[wolf]=\"howl\"","```","","### Working with dictionaries","","```bash","echo ${sounds[dog]} # Dog's sound","echo ${sounds[@]}   # All values","echo ${!sounds[@]}  # All keys","echo ${#sounds[@]}  # Number of elements","unset sounds[dog]   # Delete dog","```","","### Iteration","","```bash","for val in \"${sounds[@]}\"; do","    echo $val","done","```","","---","","```bash","for key in \"${!sounds[@]}\"; do","    echo $key","done","```","","## Bash Conditionals","","### Integer conditions","","| Condition           | Description                                 |","| ------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |","| `[[ NUM -eq NUM ]]` | \u003cyel\u003eEq\u003c/yel\u003eual                            |","| `[[ NUM -ne NUM ]]` | \u003cyel\u003eN\u003c/yel\u003eot \u003cyel\u003ee\u003c/yel\u003equal             |","| `[[ NUM -lt NUM ]]` | \u003cyel\u003eL\u003c/yel\u003eess \u003cyel\u003et\u003c/yel\u003ehan             |","| `[[ NUM -le NUM ]]` | \u003cyel\u003eL\u003c/yel\u003eess than or \u003cyel\u003ee\u003c/yel\u003equal    |","| `[[ NUM -gt NUM ]]` | \u003cyel\u003eG\u003c/yel\u003ereater \u003cyel\u003et\u003c/yel\u003ehan          |","| `[[ NUM -ge NUM ]]` | \u003cyel\u003eG\u003c/yel\u003ereater than or \u003cyel\u003ee\u003c/yel\u003equal |","| `(( NUM \u003c NUM ))`   | Less than                                   |","| `(( NUM \u003c= NUM ))`  | Less than or equal                          |","| `(( NUM \u003e NUM ))`   | Greater than                                |","| `(( NUM \u003e= NUM ))`  | Greater than or equal                       |","","### String conditions","","| Condition          | Description                 |","| ------------------ | --------------------------- |","| `[[ -z STR ]]`     | Empty string                |","| `[[ -n STR ]]`     | \u003cyel\u003eN\u003c/yel\u003eot empty string |","| `[[ STR == STR ]]` | Equal                       |","| `[[ STR = STR ]]`  | Equal (Same above)          |","| `[[ STR \u003c STR ]]`  | Less than _(ASCII)_         |","| `[[ STR \u003e STR ]]`  | Greater than _(ASCII)_      |","| `[[ STR != STR ]]` | Not Equal                   |","| `[[ STR =~ STR ]]` | Regexp                      |","","### Example {.row-span-3}","","#### String","","```bash","if [[ -z \"$string\" ]]; then","    echo \"String is empty\"","elif [[ -n \"$string\" ]]; then","    echo \"String is not empty\"","else","    echo \"This never happens\"","fi","```","","#### Combinations","","```bash","if [[ X \u0026\u0026 Y ]]; then","    ...","fi","```","","#### Equal","","```bash","if [[ \"$A\" == \"$B\" ]]; then","    ...","fi","```","","#### Regex","","```bash","if [[ '1. abc' =~ ([a-z]+) ]]; then","    echo ${BASH_REMATCH[1]}","fi","```","","#### Smaller","","```bash","if (( $a \u003c $b )); then","   echo \"$a is smaller than $b\"","fi","```","","#### Exists","","```bash","if [[ -e \"file.txt\" ]]; then","    echo \"file exists\"","fi","```","","### File conditions {.row-span-2}","","| Condition         | Description                            |","| ----------------- | -------------------------------------- |","| `[[ -e FILE ]]`   | \u003cyel\u003eE\u003c/yel\u003exists                      |","| `[[ -d FILE ]]`   | \u003cyel\u003eD\u003c/yel\u003eirectory                   |","| `[[ -f FILE ]]`   | \u003cyel\u003eF\u003c/yel\u003eile                        |","| `[[ -h FILE ]]`   | Symlink                                |","| `[[ -s FILE ]]`   | Size is \u003e 0 bytes                      |","| `[[ -r FILE ]]`   | \u003cyel\u003eR\u003c/yel\u003eeadable                    |","| `[[ -w FILE ]]`   | \u003cyel\u003eW\u003c/yel\u003eritable                    |","| `[[ -x FILE ]]`   | Executable                             |","| `[[ f1 -nt f2 ]]` | f1 \u003cyel\u003en\u003c/yel\u003eewer \u003cyel\u003et\u003c/yel\u003ehan f2 |","| `[[ f1 -ot f2 ]]` | f2 \u003cyel\u003eo\u003c/yel\u003elder \u003cyel\u003et\u003c/yel\u003ehan f1 |","| `[[ f1 -ef f2 ]]` | Same files                             |","","### More conditions","","| Condition            | Description          |","| -------------------- | -------------------- | ----- | --- |","| `[[ -o noclobber ]]` | If OPTION is enabled |","| `[[ ! EXPR ]]`       | Not                  |","| `[[ X \u0026\u0026 Y ]]`       | And                  |","| `[[ X                |                      | Y ]]` | Or  |","","### logical and, or","","```bash","if [ \"$1\" = 'y' -a $2 -gt 0 ]; then","    echo \"yes\"","fi","","if [ \"$1\" = 'n' -o $2 -lt 0 ]; then","    echo \"no\"","fi","```","","## Bash Loops","","### Basic for loop","","```bash","for i in /etc/rc.*; do","    echo $i","done","```","","### C-like for loop","","```bash","for ((i = 0 ; i \u003c 100 ; i++)); do","    echo $i","done","```","","### Ranges {.row-span-2}","","```bash","for i in {1..5}; do","    echo \"Welcome $i\"","done","```","","#### With step size","","```bash","for i in {5..50..5}; do","    echo \"Welcome $i\"","done","```","","### Continue","","```bash {data=3,5}","for number in $(seq 1 3); do","    if [[ $number == 2 ]]; then","        continue;","    fi","    echo \"$number\"","done","```","","### Break","","```bash","for number in $(seq 1 3); do","    if [[ $number == 2 ]]; then","        # Skip entire rest of loop.","        break;","    fi","    # This will only print 1","    echo \"$number\"","done","```","","### Until","","```bash","count=0","until [ $count -gt 10 ]; do","    echo \"$count\"","    ((count++))","done","```","","### While with increment","","```bash","i=1","while [[ $i -lt 4 ]]; do","    echo \"Number: $i\"","    ((i++))","done","```","","### While with decrement","","```bash","i=3","while [[ $i -gt 0 ]]; do","    echo \"Number: $i\"","    ((i--))","done","```","","#### Combined with test","","```bash","i=3","while ((i--)); do","    echo \"Number: $i\"","done","```","","### Forever","","```bash","while true; do","    # here is some code.","done","```","","### Forever (shorthand)","","```bash","while :; do","    # here is some code.","done","```","","### Reading lines","","```bash","while read line; do","    echo $line","done \u003c file.txt","```","","## Bash Functions","","### Defining functions","","```bash","myfunc() {","    echo \"hello $1\"","}","```","","```bash","# Same as above (alternate syntax)","function myfunc() {","    echo \"hello $1\"","}","```","","```bash","myfunc \"John\"","```","","### Returning values","","```bash","myfunc() {","    local myresult='some value'","    echo $myresult","}","```","","```bash","result=\"$(myfunc)\"","```","","### Raising errors","","```bash","myfunc() {","    return 1","}","```","","```bash","if myfunc; then","    echo \"success\"","else","    echo \"failure\"","fi","```","","## Bash Options {.cols-2}","","### Options","","```bash","# Avoid overlay files","# (echo \"hi\" \u003e foo)","set -o noclobber","","# Used to exit upon error","# avoiding cascading errors","set -o errexit","","# Unveils hidden failures","set -o pipefail","","# Exposes unset variables","set -o nounset","```","","### Glob options","","```bash","# Non-matching globs are removed","# ('*.foo' =\u003e '')","shopt -s nullglob","","# Non-matching globs throw errors","shopt -s failglob","","# Case insensitive globs","shopt -s nocaseglob","","# Wildcards match dotfiles","# (\"*.sh\" =\u003e \".foo.sh\")","shopt -s dotglob","","# Allow ** for recursive matches","# ('lib/**/*.rb' =\u003e 'lib/a/b/c.rb')","shopt -s globstar","```","","## Bash History {.cols-2}","","### Commands","","| Command               | Description                               |","| --------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |","| `history`             | Show history                              |","| `sudo !!`             | Run the previous command with sudo        |","| `shopt -s histverify` | Don't execute expanded result immediately |","","### Expansions","","| Expression   | Description                                          |","| ------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- |","| `!$`         | Expand last parameter of most recent command         |","| `!*`         | Expand all parameters of most recent command         |","| `!-n`        | Expand `n`th most recent command                     |","| `!n`         | Expand `n`th command in history                      |","| `!\u003ccommand\u003e` | Expand most recent invocation of command `\u003ccommand\u003e` |","","### Operations","","| Code                 | Description                                                           |","| -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |","| `!!`                 | Execute last command again                                            |","| `!!:s/\u003cFROM\u003e/\u003cTO\u003e/`  | Replace first occurrence of `\u003cFROM\u003e` to `\u003cTO\u003e` in most recent command |","| `!!:gs/\u003cFROM\u003e/\u003cTO\u003e/` | Replace all occurrences of `\u003cFROM\u003e` to `\u003cTO\u003e` in most recent command  |","| `!$:t`               | Expand only basename from last parameter of most recent command       |","| `!$:h`               | Expand only directory from last parameter of most recent command      |","","`!!` and `!$` can be replaced with any valid expansion.","","### Slices","","| Code     | Description                                                                              |","| -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |","| `!!:n`   | Expand only `n`th token from most recent command (command is `0`; first argument is `1`) |","| `!^`     | Expand first argument from most recent command                                           |","| `!$`     | Expand last token from most recent command                                               |","| `!!:n-m` | Expand range of tokens from most recent command                                          |","| `!!:n-$` | Expand `n`th token to last from most recent command                                      |","","`!!` can be replaced with any valid expansion i.e. `!cat`, `!-2`, `!42`, etc.","","## Miscellaneous","","### Numeric calculations","","```bash","$((a + 200))      # Add 200 to $a","```","","```bash","$(($RANDOM%200))  # Random number 0..199","```","","### Subshells","","```bash","(cd somedir; echo \"I'm now in $PWD\")","pwd # still in first directory","```","","### Inspecting commands","","```bash","command -V cd","#=\u003e \"cd is a function/alias/whatever\"","```","","### Redirection {.row-span-2 .col-span-2}","","```bash","python hello.py \u003e output.txt   # stdout to (file)","python hello.py \u003e\u003e output.txt  # stdout to (file), append","python hello.py 2\u003e error.log   # stderr to (file)","python hello.py 2\u003e\u00261           # stderr to stdout","python hello.py 2\u003e/dev/null    # stderr to (null)","python hello.py \u0026\u003e/dev/null    # stdout and stderr to (null)","```","","```bash","python hello.py \u003c foo.txt      # feed foo.txt to stdin for python","```","","### Source relative","","```bash","source \"${0%/*}/../share/foo.sh\"","```","","### Directory of script","","```bash","DIR=\"${0%/*}\"","```","","### Case/switch","","```bash","case \"$1\" in","    start | up)","    vagrant up","    ;;","","    *)","    echo \"Usage: $0 {start|stop|ssh}\"","    ;;","esac","```","","### Trap errors {.col-span-2}","","```bash","trap 'echo Error at about $LINENO' ERR","```","","or","","```bash","traperr() {","    echo \"ERROR: ${BASH_SOURCE[1]} at about ${BASH_LINENO[0]}\"","}","","set -o errtrace","trap traperr ERR","```","","### printf","","```bash","printf \"Hello %s, I'm %s\" Sven Olga","#=\u003e \"Hello Sven, I'm Olga","","printf \"1 + 1 = %d\" 2","#=\u003e \"1 + 1 = 2\"","","printf \"Print a float: %f\" 2","#=\u003e \"Print a float: 2.000000\"","```","","### Getting options {.col-span-2}","","```bash","while [[ \"$1\" =~ ^- \u0026\u0026 ! \"$1\" == \"--\" ]]; do case $1 in","    -V | --version )","    echo $version","    exit","    ;;","    -s | --string )","    shift; string=$1","    ;;","    -f | --flag )","    flag=1","    ;;","esac; shift; done","if [[ \"$1\" == '--' ]]; then shift; fi","```","","### Check for command's result {.col-span-2}","","```bash","if ping -c 1 google.com; then","    echo \"It appears you have a working internet connection\"","fi","```","","### Special variables {.row-span-2}","","| Expression | Description                  |","| ---------- | ---------------------------- |","| `$?`       | Exit status of last task     |","| `$!`       | PID of last background task  |","| `$$`       | PID of shell                 |","| `$0`       | Filename of the shell script |","","See [Special parameters](http://wiki.bash-hackers.org/syntax/shellvars#special_parameters_and_shell_variables).","","### Grep check {.col-span-2}","","```bash","if grep -q 'foo' ~/.bash_history; then","    echo \"You appear to have typed 'foo' in the past\"","fi","```","","### Backslash escapes {.row-span-2}","","- \u0026nbsp;","- \\!","- \\\"","- \\#","- \\\u0026","- \\'","- \\(","- \\)","- \\,","- \\;","- \\\u003c","- \\\u003e","- \\[","- \\|","- \\\\","- \\]","- \\^","- \\{","- \\}","- \\`","- \\$","- \\*","- \\?","","{.cols-4 .marker-none}","","Escape these special characters with `\\`","","### Heredoc","","```sh","cat \u003c\u003cEND","hello world","END","```","","### Go to previous directory","","```bash","pwd # /home/user/foo","cd bar/","pwd # /home/user/foo/bar","cd -","pwd # /home/user/foo","```","","### Reading input","","```bash","echo -n \"Proceed? [y/n]: \"","read ans","echo $ans","```","","```bash","read -n 1 ans    # Just one character","```","","### Conditional execution","","```bash","git commit \u0026\u0026 git push","git commit || echo \"Commit failed\"","```","","### Strict mode","","```bash","set -euo pipefail","IFS=$'\\n\\t'","```","","See: [Unofficial bash strict mode](http://redsymbol.net/articles/unofficial-bash-strict-mode/)","","### Optional arguments","","```bash","args=(\"$@\")","args+=(foo)","args+=(bar)","echo \"${args[@]}\"","```","","Put the <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Getting Started</h2><a id="user-content-getting-started" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Getting Started" href="#getting-started"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">hello.sh</h3><a id="user-content-hellosh" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: hello.sh" href="#hellosh"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="#!/bin/bash

VAR=&quot;world&quot;
echo &quot;Hello $VAR!&quot; # =&gt; Hello world!"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#!</span>/bin/bash</span>

VAR=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>world<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Hello <span class="pl-smi">$VAR</span>!<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Hello world!</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Execute the script</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ bash hello.sh"><pre>$ bash hello.sh</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Variables</h3><a id="user-content-variables" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Variables" href="#variables"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="NAME=&quot;John&quot;

echo ${NAME}    # =&gt; John (Variables)
echo $NAME      # =&gt; John (Variables)
echo &quot;$NAME&quot;    # =&gt; John (Variables)
echo '$NAME'    # =&gt; $NAME (Exact string)
echo &quot;${NAME}!&quot; # =&gt; John! (Variables)

NAME = &quot;John&quot;   # =&gt; Error (about space)"><pre>NAME=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>John<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${NAME}</span>    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; John (Variables)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">$NAME</span>      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; John (Variables)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$NAME</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; John (Variables)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>$NAME<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; $NAME (Exact string)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${NAME}</span>!<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; John! (Variables)</span>

NAME = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>John<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Error (about space)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Comments</h3><a id="user-content-comments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Comments" href="#comments"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# This is an inline Bash comment."><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> This is an inline Bash comment.</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=": '
This is a
very neat comment
in bash
'"><pre><span class="pl-c1">:</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">This is a</span>
<span class="pl-s">very neat comment</span>
<span class="pl-s">in bash</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Multi-line comments use <code>:'</code> to open and <code>'</code> to close</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Arguments {.row-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-arguments-row-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Arguments {.row-span-2}" href="#arguments-row-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Expression</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>$1</code> … <code>$9</code></td>
<td>Parameter 1 ... 9</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$0</code></td>
<td>Name of the script itself</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$1</code></td>
<td>First argument</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${10}</code></td>
<td>Positional parameter 10</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$#</code></td>
<td>Number of arguments</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$$</code></td>
<td>Process id of the shell</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$*</code></td>
<td>All arguments</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$@</code></td>
<td>All arguments, starting from first</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$-</code></td>
<td>Current options</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>$_</code></td>
<td>Last argument of the previous command</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get_name() {
    echo &quot;John&quot;
}

echo &quot;You are $(get_name)&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-en">get_name</span>() {
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>John<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
}

<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>You are <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>get_name<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if [[ -z &quot;$string&quot; ]]; then
    echo &quot;String is empty&quot;
elif [[ -n &quot;$string&quot; ]]; then
    echo &quot;String is not empty&quot;
fi"><pre><span class="pl-k">if</span> [[ <span class="pl-k">-z</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$string</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>String is empty<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">elif</span> [[ <span class="pl-k">-n</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$string</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>String is not empty<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">fi</span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="echo {A,B}.js"><pre><span class="pl-c1">echo</span> {A,B}.js</pre></div>
<hr>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Expression</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>{A,B}</code></td>
<td>Same as <code>A B</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>{A,B}.js</code></td>
<td>Same as <code>A.js B.js</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>{1..5}</code></td>
<td>Same as <code>1 2 3 4 5</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>

<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Code</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO%suffix}</code></td>
<td>Remove suffix</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO#prefix}</code></td>
<td>Remove prefix</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO%%suffix}</code></td>
<td>Remove long suffix</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO##prefix}</code></td>
<td>Remove long prefix</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO/from/to}</code></td>
<td>Replace first match</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO//from/to}</code></td>
<td>Replace all</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO/%from/to}</code></td>
<td>Replace suffix</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO/#from/to}</code></td>
<td>Replace prefix</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Substrings</h4><a id="user-content-substrings" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Substrings" href="#substrings"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Expression</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO:-val}</code></td>
<td><code>$FOO</code>, or <code>val</code> if unset</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO:=val}</code></td>
<td>Set <code>$FOO</code> to <code>val</code> if unset</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO:+val}</code></td>
<td><code>val</code> if <code>$FOO</code> is set</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>${FOO:?message}</code></td>
<td>Show message and exit if <code>$FOO</code> is unset</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="echo ${food:-Cake}  #=&gt; $food or &quot;Cake&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${food<span class="pl-k">:-</span>Cake}</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>=&gt; $food or "Cake"</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="STR=&quot;/path/to/foo.cpp&quot;
echo ${STR%.cpp}    # /path/to/foo
echo ${STR%.cpp}.o  # /path/to/foo.o
echo ${STR%/*}      # /path/to

echo ${STR##*.}     # cpp (extension)
echo ${STR##*/}     # foo.cpp (basepath)

echo ${STR#*/}      # path/to/foo.cpp
echo ${STR##*/}     # foo.cpp

echo ${STR/foo/bar} # /path/to/bar.cpp"><pre>STR=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>/path/to/foo.cpp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">%</span>.cpp}</span>    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> /path/to/foo</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">%</span>.cpp}</span>.o  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> /path/to/foo.o</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">%/*</span>}</span>      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> /path/to</span>

<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">##*</span>.}</span>     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> cpp (extension)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">##*/</span>}</span>     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> foo.cpp (basepath)</span>

<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">#*/</span>}</span>      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> path/to/foo.cpp</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">##*/</span>}</span>     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> foo.cpp</span>

<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR<span class="pl-k">/</span>foo<span class="pl-k">/</span>bar}</span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> /path/to/bar.cpp</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Slicing</h3><a id="user-content-slicing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Slicing" href="#slicing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="name=&quot;John&quot;
echo ${name}           # =&gt; John
echo ${name:0:2}       # =&gt; Jo
echo ${name::2}        # =&gt; Jo
echo ${name::-1}       # =&gt; Joh
echo ${name:(-1)}      # =&gt; n
echo ${name:(-2)}      # =&gt; hn
echo ${name:(-2):2}    # =&gt; hn

length=2
echo ${name:0:length}  # =&gt; Jo"><pre>name=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>John<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name}</span>           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; John</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name<span class="pl-k">:</span>0<span class="pl-k">:</span>2}</span>       <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Jo</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name<span class="pl-k">::</span>2}</span>        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Jo</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name<span class="pl-k">::-</span>1}</span>       <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Joh</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name<span class="pl-k">:</span>(-1)}</span>      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; n</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name<span class="pl-k">:</span>(-2)}</span>      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; hn</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name<span class="pl-k">:</span>(-2)<span class="pl-k">:</span>2}</span>    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; hn</span>

length=2
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${name<span class="pl-k">:</span>0<span class="pl-k">:</span>length}</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Jo</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">See: <a href="http://wiki.bash-hackers.org/syntax/pe" rel="nofollow">Parameter expansion</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">basepath &amp; dirpath</h3><a id="user-content-basepath--dirpath" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: basepath &amp; dirpath" href="#basepath--dirpath"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="SRC=&quot;/path/to/foo.cpp&quot;"><pre>SRC=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>/path/to/foo.cpp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="BASEPATH=${SRC##*/}
echo $BASEPATH  # =&gt; &quot;foo.cpp&quot;


DIRPATH=${SRC%$BASEPATH}
echo $DIRPATH   # =&gt; &quot;/path/to/&quot;"><pre>BASEPATH=<span class="pl-smi">${SRC<span class="pl-k">##*/</span>}</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">$BASEPATH</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; "foo.cpp"</span>


DIRPATH=<span class="pl-smi">${SRC<span class="pl-k">%</span><span class="pl-smi">$BASEPATH</span>}</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">$DIRPATH</span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; "/path/to/"</span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="STR=&quot;HELLO WORLD!&quot;
echo ${STR,}   # =&gt; hELLO WORLD!
echo ${STR,,}  # =&gt; hello world!

STR=&quot;hello world!&quot;
echo ${STR^}   # =&gt; Hello world!
echo ${STR^^}  # =&gt; HELLO WORLD!

ARR=(hello World)
echo &quot;${ARR[@],}&quot; # =&gt; hello world
echo &quot;${ARR[@]^}&quot; # =&gt; Hello World"><pre>STR=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>HELLO WORLD!<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR,}</span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; hELLO WORLD!</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR,,}</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; hello world!</span>

STR=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>hello world!<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR^}</span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Hello world!</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${STR^^}</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; HELLO WORLD!</span>

ARR=(hello World)
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${ARR[@],}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; hello world</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${ARR[@]^}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Hello World</span></pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Defining arrays</h3><a id="user-content-defining-arrays" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Defining arrays" href="#defining-arrays"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Fruits=('Apple' 'Banana' 'Orange')

Fruits[0]=&quot;Apple&quot;
Fruits[1]=&quot;Banana&quot;
Fruits[2]=&quot;Orange&quot;

ARRAY1=(foo{1..2}) # =&gt; foo1 foo2
ARRAY2=({A..D})    # =&gt; A B C D

# Merge =&gt; foo1 foo2 A B C D
ARRAY3=(${ARRAY1[@]} ${ARRAY2[@]})

# declare construct
declare -a Numbers=(1 2 3)
Numbers+=(4 5) # Append =&gt; 1 2 3 4 5"><pre>Fruits=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Apple<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Banana<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Orange<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)

Fruits[0]=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Apple<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
Fruits[1]=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Banana<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
Fruits[2]=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Orange<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

ARRAY1=(foo{1..2}) <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; foo1 foo2</span>
ARRAY2=({A..D})    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; A B C D</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Merge =&gt; foo1 foo2 A B C D</span>
ARRAY3=(<span class="pl-smi">${ARRAY1[@]}</span> <span class="pl-smi">${ARRAY2[@]}</span>)

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> declare construct</span>
<span class="pl-k">declare</span> -a Numbers=(1 2 3)
Numbers+=(4 5) <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Append =&gt; 1 2 3 4 5</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Indexing</h3><a id="user-content-indexing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Indexing" href="#indexing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Fruits=('Apple' 'Banana' 'Orange')

for e in &quot;${Fruits[@]}&quot;; do

done"><pre>Fruits=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Apple<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Banana<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Orange<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Fruits=(&quot;${Fruits[@]}&quot; &quot;Watermelon&quot;)     # Push
Fruits+=('Watermelon')                   # Also Push
Fruits=( ${Fruits[@]/Ap*/} )             # Remove by regex match
unset Fruits[2]                          # Remove one item
Fruits=(&quot;${Fruits[@]}&quot;)                  # Duplicate
Fruits=(&quot;${Fruits[@]}&quot; &quot;${Veggies[@]}&quot;)  # Concatenate
lines=(`cat &quot;logfile&quot;`)                  # Read from file"><pre>Fruits=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${Fruits[@]}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Watermelon<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>)     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Push</span>
Fruits+=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Watermelon<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)                   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Also Push</span>
Fruits=( <span class="pl-smi">${Fruits[@]<span class="pl-k">/</span>Ap<span class="pl-k">*/</span>}</span> )             <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove by regex match</span>
<span class="pl-c1">unset</span> Fruits[2]                          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Remove one item</span>
Fruits=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${Fruits[@]}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>)                  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Duplicate</span>
Fruits=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${Fruits[@]}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${Veggies[@]}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>)  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Concatenate</span>
lines=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">`</span>cat <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>logfile<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span><span class="pl-pds">`</span></span>)                  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Read from file</span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="function extract()
{
    local -n myarray=$1
    local idx=$2
    echo &quot;${myarray[$idx]}&quot;
}
Fruits=('Apple' 'Banana' 'Orange')
extract Fruits 2     # =&gt; Orangle"><pre><span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-en">extract()</span>
{
    <span class="pl-k">local</span> -n myarray=<span class="pl-smi">$1</span>
    <span class="pl-k">local</span> idx=<span class="pl-smi">$2</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${myarray[$idx]}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
}
Fruits=(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Apple<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Banana<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Orange<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)
extract Fruits 2     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> =&gt; Orangle</span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sounds[dog]=&quot;bark&quot;
sounds[cow]=&quot;moo&quot;

sounds[bird]=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>tweet<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="echo ${sounds[dog]} # Dog's sound
echo ${sounds[@]}   # All values
echo ${!sounds[@]}  # All keys
echo ${#sounds[@]}  # Number of elements
unset sounds[dog]   # Delete dog"><pre><span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${sounds[dog]}</span> <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Dog's sound</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${sounds[@]}</span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> All values</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${<span class="pl-k">!</span>sounds[@]}</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> All keys</span>
<span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-smi">${<span class="pl-k">#</span>sounds[@]}</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Number of elements</span>
<span class="pl-c1">unset</span> sounds[dog]   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Delete dog</span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="for val in &quot;${sounds[@]}&quot;; do

done"><pre><span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-smi">val</span> <span class="pl-k">in</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${sounds[@]}</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span><span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">do</span>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Bash Conditionals</h2><a id="user-content-bash-conditionals" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Bash Conditionals" href="#bash-conditionals"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Integer conditions</h3><a id="user-content-integer-conditions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Integer conditions" href="#integer-conditions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Condition</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ NUM -eq NUM ]]</code></td>
<td>Equal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ NUM -ne NUM ]]</code></td>
<td>Not equal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ NUM -lt NUM ]]</code></td>
<td>Less than</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ NUM -le NUM ]]</code></td>
<td>Less than or equal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ NUM -gt NUM ]]</code></td>
<td>Greater than</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ NUM -ge NUM ]]</code></td>
<td>Greater than or equal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>(( NUM &lt; NUM ))</code></td>
<td>Less than</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>(( NUM &lt;= NUM ))</code></td>
<td>Less than or equal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>(( NUM &gt; NUM ))</code></td>
<td>Greater than</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>(( NUM &gt;= NUM ))</code></td>
<td>Greater than or equal</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">String conditions</h3><a id="user-content-string-conditions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: String conditions" href="#string-conditions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Condition</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -z STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Empty string</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -n STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Not empty string</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ STR == STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Equal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ STR = STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Equal (Same above)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ STR &lt; STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Less than <em>(ASCII)</em></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ STR &gt; STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Greater than <em>(ASCII)</em></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ STR != STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Not Equal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ STR =~ STR ]]</code></td>
<td>Regexp</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example {.row-span-3}</h3><a id="user-content-example-row-span-3" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example {.row-span-3}" href="#example-row-span-3"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">String</h4><a id="user-content-string" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: String" href="#string"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if [[ -z &quot;$string&quot; ]]; then
    echo &quot;String is empty&quot;
elif [[ -n &quot;$string&quot; ]]; then
    echo &quot;String is not empty&quot;
else
    echo &quot;This never happens&quot;
fi"><pre><span class="pl-k">if</span> [[ <span class="pl-k">-z</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$string</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>String is empty<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">elif</span> [[ <span class="pl-k">-n</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$string</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>String is not empty<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">else</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>This never happens<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">fi</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Combinations</h4><a id="user-content-combinations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Combinations" href="#combinations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if [[ '1. abc' =~ ([a-z]+) ]]; then

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if (( $a &lt; $b )); then
   echo &quot;$a is smaller than $b&quot;
fi"><pre><span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">((</span> <span class="pl-smi">$a</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span> <span class="pl-smi">$b</span> <span class="pl-pds">))</span></span><span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
   <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$a</span> is smaller than <span class="pl-smi">$b</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">fi</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Exists</h4><a id="user-content-exists" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Exists" href="#exists"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if [[ -e &quot;file.txt&quot; ]]; then
    echo &quot;file exists&quot;
fi"><pre><span class="pl-k">if</span> [[ <span class="pl-k">-e</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>file.txt<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">File conditions {.row-span-2}</h3><a id="user-content-file-conditions-row-span-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: File conditions {.row-span-2}" href="#file-conditions-row-span-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Condition</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -e FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>Exists</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -d FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>Directory</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -f FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>File</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -h FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>Symlink</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -s FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>Size is &gt; 0 bytes</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -r FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>Readable</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -w FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>Writable</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ -x FILE ]]</code></td>
<td>Executable</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ f1 -nt f2 ]]</code></td>
<td>f1 newer than f2</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ f1 -ot f2 ]]</code></td>
<td>f2 older than f1</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>[[ f1 -ef f2 ]]</code></td>
<td>Same files</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">More conditions</h3><a id="user-content-more-conditions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: More conditions" href="#more-conditions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">| Condition            | Description          |
| -------------------- | -------------------- | ----- | --- |
| <code>[[ -o noclobber ]]</code> | If OPTION is enabled |
| <code>[[ ! EXPR ]]</code>       | Not                  |
| <code>[[ X &amp;&amp; Y ]]</code>       | And                  |
| <code>[[ X                |                      | Y ]]</code> | Or  |</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">logical and, or</h3><a id="user-content-logical-and-or" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: logical and, or" href="#logical-and-or"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="if [ &quot;$1&quot; = 'y' -a $2 -gt 0 ]; then
    echo &quot;yes&quot;
fi

if [ &quot;$1&quot; = 'n' -o $2 -lt 0 ]; then
    echo &quot;no&quot;
fi"><pre><span class="pl-k">if</span> [ <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$1</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>y<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-a</span> <span class="pl-smi">$2</span> <span class="pl-k">-gt</span> 0 ]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>yes<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">fi</span>

<span class="pl-k">if</span> [ <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$1</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>n<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-o</span> <span class="pl-smi">$2</span> <span class="pl-k">-lt</span> 0 ]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>no<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">fi</span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="for ((i = 0 ; i &lt; 100 ; i++)); do

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Continue</h3><a id="user-content-continue" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Continue" href="#continue"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="for number in $(seq 1 3); do
    if [[ $number == 2 ]]; then
        continue;
    fi
    echo &quot;$number&quot;
done"><pre><span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-smi">number</span> <span class="pl-k">in</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>seq 1 3<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span><span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">do</span>
    <span class="pl-k">if</span> [[ <span class="pl-smi">$number</span> <span class="pl-k">==</span> 2 ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
        <span class="pl-k">continue</span><span class="pl-k">;</span>
    <span class="pl-k">fi</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$number</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">done</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Break</h3><a id="user-content-break" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Break" href="#break"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="for number in $(seq 1 3); do
    if [[ $number == 2 ]]; then
        # Skip entire rest of loop.
        break;
    fi
    # This will only print 1
    echo &quot;$number&quot;
done"><pre><span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-smi">number</span> <span class="pl-k">in</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>seq 1 3<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span><span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">do</span>
    <span class="pl-k">if</span> [[ <span class="pl-smi">$number</span> <span class="pl-k">==</span> 2 ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">then</span>
        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Skip entire rest of loop.</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">break</span><span class="pl-k">;</span>
    <span class="pl-k">fi</span>
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> This will only print 1</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$number</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">done</span></pre></div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="count=0
until [ $count -gt 10 ]; do
    echo &quot;$count&quot;
    ((count++))
done"><pre>count=0
<span class="pl-k">until</span> [ <span class="pl-smi">$count</span> <span class="pl-k">-gt</span> 10 ]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">do</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$count</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
    <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">((</span>count<span class="pl-k">++</span><span class="pl-pds">))</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">done</span></pre></div>

while [[ $i -lt 4 ]]; do
    echo &quot;Number: $i&quot;
    ((i++))
done"><pre>i=1
<span class="pl-k">while</span> [[ <span class="pl-smi">$i</span> <span class="pl-k">-lt</span> 4 ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">do</span>

    <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">((</span>i<span class="pl-k">++</span><span class="pl-pds">))</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">done</span></pre></div>

while [[ $i -gt 0 ]]; do
    echo &quot;Number: $i&quot;
    ((i--))
done"><pre>i=3
<span class="pl-k">while</span> [[ <span class="pl-smi">$i</span> <span class="pl-k">-gt</span> 0 ]]<span class="pl-k">;</span> <span class="pl-k">do</span>

<span class="pl-k">done</span></pre></div>

<span class="pl-k">fi</span></pre></div>
